/* ---------------- RNG deterministico ( solo per ge nera re i dati fittizi) ---------------- */
function mul berry 32( see d) {
  let a = seed >>> 0;
  return function () {
    a |= 0; a = (a + 0x6D2B 79F5) | 0;
    let t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a);
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    return ( (t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}

const rndInt = ( r, a, b) => a + Math.floor( r() * ( b - a + 1));

/* ---------------- Crittografia: SHA-1 / HMAC / TOTP (impleme ntazione pura) ---------
   Nota: SHA-1 qui è usato S OLO pe r l'HMAC di TOTP ( standard RFC 6238) e come hash
   NON sicuro di comodità per i token nel demo. In produzione: passw ord con
   Argon2id/ bcrypt, tok en confrontati con ha sh a tempo costa nte lato serve r. */
const e nc = ne w TextEncoder( );
function sha1B ytes(m sgByte s) {
  const ml = m sgByte s.lengt h, total = (Math.ceil ((ml + 9) / 64)) * 64;
  const buf = ne w Uint8Array(total );
  buf. set(msgB ytes); buf[ml] = 0x 80;
  const dv = ne w DataV iew( buf.buffer);
  dv.setUint32(t otal - 8, Math.floor(ml / 536870912)) ;
  dv.setUint32(t otal - 4, (ml << 3) >>> 0);
  let h0 = 0x67452301, h1 = 0xE FCDAB 89, h2 = 0x 98B ADCFE, h3 = 0x10325476, h4 = 0xC 3D2E1F0;
  const w = ne w Uint32Array( 80);
  const rotl = (x, n) => ((x << n) | (x >>> (32 - n)) ) >>> 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < total ; i += 64) {
    for (let j = 0; j < 16; j++) w[j] = dv.getUint32(i + j * 4);
    for (let j = 16; j < 80; j ++) w[j] = rotl ((w[j - 3] ^ w[j - 8] ^ w[j - 14] ^ w[ j - 16]) >>> 0, 1);
    let a = h0, b = h1, c = h2, d = h3, e = h4;
    for (let j = 0; j < 80; j++) {
      let f, k;
      if (j < 20) { f = (b & c) | (~b & d); k = 0x 5A827999; }
      else if (j < 40) { f = b ^ c ^ d; k = 0x 6ED9EBA1; }
      else if (j < 60) { f = ( b & c) | (b & d) | (c & d); k = 0x 8F1BBCDC; }
      else { f = b ^ c ^ d; k = 0xCA 62C1D6; }
      const t = (rotl(a, 5) + (( (f >>> 0) + e) >>> 0) + k + w[j]) >>> 0;
      e = d; d = c; c = rot l(b, 30); b = a; a = t;
    }
    h0 = ( h0 + a) >>> 0; h1 = (h1 + b) >>> 0; h2 = (h2 + c) >>> 0; h3 = (h3 + d) >>> 0; h4 = ( h4 + e) >>> 0;
  }
  const out = new U int 8Array( 20), odv = ne w DataView( out.buffer);
  odv.setU int 32(0, h0); odv.setUint32( 4, h1); odv.set Uint32( 8, h2) ; odv.setU int 32(12, h3); odv.set Uint 32(16, h4);
  return out;
}
function _cat(a, b) { const r = new Uint8A rray (a.length + b.length); r.set(a); r.set( b, a.lengt h); ret urn r; }
function hmacS ha1( keyByte s, msgByte s) {
  let key = keyB ytes;
  if (key.le ngth > 64) ke y = sha 1Bytes(key) ;
  const ipad = ne w Uint8Array( 64), opa d = new U int 8Array( 64);
  for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) { const k b = i < key.le ngth ? key[ i] : 0; ipa d[i] = k b ^ 0x 36; opa d[i] = kb ^ 0x 5c; }
  return sha1B ytes(_cat( opa d, sha1B ytes(_cat(ipa d, msgByte s)))) ;
}
const sha 1hex = s => byte sToHex(sha 1Byte s(enc.e ncode( s)) );

const B 32 = "ABCDE FGHIJKLM NOP QRSTUV WXYZ234567";
function ba se32Decode (s) {
  s = String( s).re place(/ =+$/, "").toU pperCase().re place(/[ ^A-Z2-7]/g, "") ;
  let bits = 0, val = 0; const out = [];
  for (const ch of s) { val = (val << 5) | B32. indexOf(ch) ; bit s += 5; if ( bits >= 8) { out.push(( val >>> (bits - 8)) & 0x ff); bit s -= 8; } }
  return ne w Uint8Array(out);
}
function ba se32Enc ode( byte s) {
  let bits = 0, val = 0, out = "";
  for (const b of bytes) { val = (val << 8) | b; bits += 8; w hile ( bits >= 5) { out += B 32[(v al >>> ( bit s - 5)) & 31]; bits -= 5; } }
  if (bit s > 0) out += B32[(val << (5 - bit s)) & 31];
  return out;
}
function totpCode( secretB 32, counter) {
  const msg = ne w Uint8Array( 8), dv = ne w DataVie w(msg.buffer);
  dv.setUint32( 0, Math.fl oor(counter / 0x100000000) ); dv.setUint32( 4, counter >>> 0) ;
  const mac = hmacS ha1( ba se32Decode (secretB 32), msg);
  const off = mac[19] & 0x 0f;
  const code = ((mac[off] & 0x 7f) << 24 | mac[off + 1] << 16 | mac[off + 2] << 8 | mac[off + 3]) >>> 0;
  return code ;
}
function totpNow( secretB32, atMs) {
  const t = Math.floor((atMs || Date.now() ) / 1000);
  return String(tot pCode(secretB32, Math.fl oor(t / 30)) % 1e6). padStart( 6, "0") ;
}
function totpVe rify( secretB32, code) {
  const c = String(code || "").re place(/\ s/g, "");
  if (!/ ^\d{6} $/.test(c)) ret urn fal se;
  const t = Math.floor( Date.now() / 1000) ;
  // fine stra ±1 periodo ( 30 s) pe r tolle rare piccol i scostamenti di orologio
  for (const off of [-1, 0, 1]) {
    if (String(tot pCode (secretB 32, Math.floor(t / 30) + off) % 1e 6).pa dStart( 6, "0") === c) return true;
  }
  return fal se;
}

  if (v >= 8) return { color: "# 2C6E49", la bel: " Ottimo" };

  return { color: "#A 63A 2B", labe l: "G ravi crit icità" };
}

/* ---------------- Statistiche, media e cla ssific he ---------------- */
function publishe dReview s(ent ityId) {
  return DB( ).revie ws. filter(x => x.entityI d === e ntityId && x.status === " publ ishe d");

function statsFor(e ntityId) {
  const rev s = publ ishedRevie ws(entityI d);
  const ent = DB().e ntitie s.find(e => e.id === e ntityId) ;
  const cat = ent ? catById(ent.categoryId) : null;
  const n = rev s.le ngth;
  const mean = n ? rev s.re duce(( s, x) => s + x.score, 0) / n : null;
  const crit = { };
  if (cat) cat.criteria.forEach(c => {
    const vals = rev s.map(x => x.criteria[c.id]).filter(v => v != null);
    crit[c.id] = vals.le ngth ? val s.re duce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / val s.lengt h : null ;
  });
  return { n: n, mean: mea n, crit: crit, sufficie nt: n >= DB(). setting s.minRevie ws, e nt: ent, cat: cat };
}
/* Statistic he de i prezzi dichiarati: mediana e interv all o sull' unità più ric orrente
   (evita di mescolare, per esempio, canoni me nsili e prezzi di vendita). */
function priceStats(e ntityId) {
  const rev s = publ ishedRevie ws(entityI d).filter( r => r.price && r.price.amount > 0);
  if (!re vs.le ngth) return null;
  const by Unit = {} ;
  revs.forEach( r => { ( byU nit[r. price.unit] = by Unit[r.price.unit] || []).push(r. price.amount); });
  let unit = null, arr = nul l;
  Object.keys(by Unit).forEach( u => { if (!arr || byU nit[u].le ngth > arr.lengt h) { unit = u; a rr = by Unit[u]; } }) ;
  arr.sort((a, b) => a - b) ;
  const median = arr.lengt h % 2 ? arr[(arr.length - 1) / 2] : (arr[a rr.le ngt h / 2 - 1] + a rr[a rr.le ngth / 2]) / 2;
  return { unit: unit, min: arr[0], max: arr[arr.length - 1], median: me dia n, n: a rr.le ngth } ;
}
function categoryPrior(catId) {
  const means = DB().e ntitie s.filte r(e => e.categoryI d === catId && e.status === " publ ishe d")
    .map(e => { const s = stat sFor(e.id).mean; return s; }). filte r(v => v != null );
  return means.le ngth ? mea ns.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / means.lengt h : 6. 0;
}
/* Media ponderata (cre dibile ) alla Baye s: WR = ( n·R + m·C) / (n + m)
   m = soglia m inima di recensioni; C = media della categoria.
   Una sola rece nsione (anc he massima) non può conquistare il primo posto. */
function weig hte dScore( stats, prior, m) {
  if (stats. n === 0) ret urn nul l;
  const mm = m == null ? DB( ).settings.minRev iew s : m;
  return (stat s.n * stats.mean + mm * prior) / ( stats.n + mm);
}
function ra nking For(catId, opt s) {
  const o = opt s || {};
  const prior = categoryPrior(catId);
  const rows = DB ().entit ies
    .filter(e => e.categoryI d === catId && e.status === " published" && (!o.zoneI d || e.zoneId === o.zoneI d))
    .map(e => {
      const st = stat sFor(e.id);
      return { e nt: e, n: st. n, mean: st.mea n, weig hted: weig htedScore( st, prior), sufficie nt: st.sufficie nt };
    });
  rows.sort((a, b) => {
    // prima le ent ità con dati sufficie nti, poi il punteggio ponde rato
    if (!!a.sufficie nt !== !!b. sufficient) return a.sufficie nt ? -1 : 1;
    const wa = a.we ighte d == nul l ? -1 : a.we ighte d, wb = b.w eig hted == null ? -1 : b.weig hte d;
    return wb - wa;
  });
  return rows;
}
function top3For(catId, zoneId) {
  const rows = ra nk ing For(catId, { zoneI d: zoneId });
  return rows. slice( 0, 3).filter(x => x.n > 0);
}
function zone Summa ry(zoneId) {
  return CATS.map(c => ({ cat: c, row s: top3For(c.id, zoneI d) })) ;
}

function validateRev iewPayl oa d(p, cat) {
  const num = v => ( v === " " || v == null ) ? nul l : Numbe r(v) ;
  const score = num (p.sc ore);
  if (score == null || score < 0 || score > 10 || (score * 2) % 1 !== 0) return { error: "Puntegg io complessiv o non v alido ( 0– 10, ste p 0, 5)." };
  for (const c of cat.criteria) {
    const v = num( p.criteria[c.id]);
    if (v == null || v < 0 || v > 10 || (v * 2) % 1 !== 0) ret urn { error: "Val utazione manca nte o non valida pe r: " + c.label + "." };
  }
  if (!String (p. pros || "").trim()) return { e rror: "I ndica almeno un aspetto positiv o." };
  if (p. priceAm ount !== "" && p. priceAm ount != null) {
    const amt = Numbe r( p.priceAmount);
    if (!isFinite(amt) || amt <= 0 || amt > 100000000) return { e rror: "Im port o del prezzo non val ido (tra 1 € e 100.000.000 € )." };
    if (cat.priceU nits && cat.priceU nit s.index Of( p.priceU nit) < 0) return { error: "Periodo del prezzo non v alido per que sta cate goria." };
  }
  if (!String (p.text || "").trim() || String (p.text).trim().le ngt h < 100) ret urn { error: "La desc rizione dell'e sperie nza de ve essere di alme no 100 caratteri." };
  if (!p.hone sty) ret urn { error: "Dev i conferma re la dichiarazione sulla veridicità e sull'a sse nza di dati personali di terz i. " };
  if (String( p.hp || "").trim()) return { error: "R ichie sta rifiutata (cont rol lo a nti-bot)." } ; // honey pot
  return { ok: true } ;
}

function starPat hD(cx, cy, R) {
  const r = R * 0. 42;
  let d = "";
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
    const ang = -Math.PI / 2 + (i * Math.PI) / 5;
    const rad = i % 2 === 0 ? R : r;
    d += (i === 0 ? "M" : " L") + (cx + ra d * Math.cos(a ng)).toFixed(2) + "," + (cy + ra d * Math. sin(ang)).toFixed(2) ;
  }
  return d + "Z" ;
}
function starsSVG( score, opt s) {

  const sta rs5 = clam p(( score == null ? 0 : score) / 2, 0, 5);
  const pct = ( stars5 / 5) * 100;
  const clipId = " sc" + Mat h.random ().toString( 36).sl ice(2, 9);
  const la bel = score == null ? " ne ssuna recensione " : fmt 1(sta rs5).re place(/, 0$/, "") + " stell e su 5 ( punteggio " + fmt1( score ) + " su 10) ";
  return '<svg class=" stars" width="' + w + '" heig ht="' + size + '" v iewB ox="0 0 ' + w + " " + size + '" role =" img" aria -labe l="' + e sc(labe l) + '" >' +
    '<defs><clipPath id="' + clipId + '"><rect x="0" y="0" width="' + ( w * pct) / 100 + ' " heig ht="' + size + '"/ ></clipPath></ de fs>' +
    '<g fill ="# D8D2C0" >' + row + </g>" +
    '<g fill ="#C 9A227" clip-pat h=" url(#' + cl ipI d + ')" >' + row + " </g></ svg >";
}

/* ---------------- Modali ( focus tra p + Esc) ---------------- */
let _modalP revFocus = nul l;
function openModal (html, opts) {
  const o = opt s || {};
  _modalPre vFoc us = docume nt.activeElement;
  $("#modal-root").innerHTML =
    '<div class="modal-overl ay" data-close =" 1" ><div cla ss="m oda l' + (o.la rge ? " moda l-lg" : " ") + '" role =" dial og" a ria-modal ="true" a ria-la bel ="' + e sc(o.la bel || "Finestra di dial ogo") + '" >' + html + " </ div ></div>";
  const ove rlay = $("#m oda l-root .modal- overlay") ;
  overlay.a ddEve ntListe ner( "mouse dow n", ev => { if (e v.target === overlay) closeModal() ; });
  const focusable s = ( ) => $$(" #modal-root a, #moda l-root button, #modal- root input, #modal-root se lect, #modal-root textarea, #modal- root [tabindex]: not([ta bindex ='-1']) ").filter(e l => !el. disa ble d && el.offsetPare nt !== null);
  const first = focusable s()[ 0];
  if (first) first.focus();
  overlay._tra p = function (ev ) {
    if (ev.key === "Tab" ) {
      const f = focusables( );
      if (!f.length) return;
      const firstEl = f[0], lastEl = f[ f.lengt h - 1];
      if (ev.shiftKey && doc ument.activeEleme nt === firstEl) { ev. preve ntDe fa ult(); la stEl.foc us( ); }
      else if (!ev. shiftKey && document.activeEleme nt === lastEl) { ev. prev nt Defa ult(); firstEl.focus(); }
    }
  };
  overlay.a ddEve ntListe ner( "key dow n", ove rlay._tra p);
}
function closeModa l() {
  const ove rlay = $("#m oda l-root .modal- overlay") ;
  if (ove rlay) { overlay.rem oveEvent Listene r(" keydow n", ove rlay._t rap) ; }
  $("#modal-root").innerHTML = " ";
  if (_modalPrev Foc us && _moda lPrev Focus.focus) { try { _modalPre vFocus. focus(); } catch (e) {} }

function pa rseHa sh( ) {
  const h = location.hash || "#/";
  const pa rts = h.slice(1).split(" ?") ;
  const seg s = part s[0]. spl it("/" ).filte r(Boolea n);
  const pa rams = {};
  if (pa rts[ 1]) pa rts[ 1].split("&" ).forEach(k v => {
    const p = kv.split( "=" );
    if (p[0]) params[ decode URIComponent( p[0])] = decode URICompone nt( p[1] || "");
  });
  return { seg s: segs, param s: pa rams } ;
}

/* ---------------- Ricerca ---------------- */
function sea rchRe sults( p) {
  const q = (p. q || "").trim().toLowe rCase() ;
  const cats = p.cat ? String(p.cat).split( ",").filter(c => catById(c)) : [];
  const zone = p.zona || "";
  const suff = p.suff === "1" ;
  const ord = p.ord || "rileva nza";
  let rows = DB ().entit ies.filter( e => e.status === " publishe d" && e.categoryId !== "zona");
  if (cats.lengt h) row s = row s.filter(e => cats.indexOf(e.categoryId) >= 0);
  if (zone ) rows = row s.filte r(e => e.zoneId === zone );
  if (q) rows = rows.filter(e =>
    (e.name + " " + (e.a ddress || "") + " " + (e.subtitl e || "") + " " + zone Name(e.zoneId)).toLowerCa se().index Of(q) >= 0);
  const pm in = p.pm in ? Numbe r( p.pmin) : nul l, pmax = p.pmax ? Numbe r( p.pmax) : nul l, punit = p. punit || "";
  if (pmin != nul l || pmax != nul l || punit) {
    rows = rows. filte r(e => DB ().review s.some( r => r.e ntityId === e.id && r.stat us === " published" &&
      r.price && r.price.amount > 0 &&
      (!punit || r.price.unit === punit) &&
      (pmin == null || r.price.amount >= pm in) &&
      (pmax == nul l || r.price.amount <= pmax)));
  }
  const priorCache = {};
  rows = rows.map(e => {
    const st = statsFor(e.id) ;
    if (!(e.categoryI d in priorCache)) priorCache[e.categoryId] = categoryPrior(e.categoryI d);
    return { e nt: e, n: st.n, mean: st.mean, weighte d: weighte dScore (st, priorCache[e.categoryI d]), sufficie nt: st.sufficie nt };
  });
  if (suff) rows = row s.filter(x => x.sufficie nt && x.n > 0);
  const cmp = {
    rilevanza: (a, b) => (b. weig hted || -1) - (a.weig hted || -1),
    puntegg io: (a, b) => (b.mea || -1) - (a.mean || -1),
    recensioni: (a, b) => b.n - .n,
    nome: (a, b) => a.ent. name.localeCom pare (b.e nt.name, "it")
  }[ord] || function () { ret urn 0; };
  rows.sort((a, b) => { const d = cmp(a, b); ret urn d !== 0 ? d : b.n - a.n; });
  return rows;
}

    if (st && st.n > 0) svg += ' <text class="z one- score " x="' + z.lx + '" y ="' + (z.ly + 13) + '" text-a nchor="middle " fill="' + scoreBa nd(st.mea n).color + '" >' + fmt1( st.mean) + "/ 10 · " + st.n + ( st.sufficient ? "" : " ( dati p ochi)") + "</text >";

  });

/* Zona di rife riment o de ll'ute nte: prefere nza tecnica sal vata sul dispositiv o ( stile cookie),
   mai associata al l'account. Le coordinate GPS se rvono solo al mome nto e ve ngono scartate. */
function prefsMyZone () {
  try { return l ocalStora ge.getItem(SITE.storageKey + ".miaZona") || null; } catch (e) { ret urn nul l; }
}
function prefsSetMyZone (zoneI d) {
  try {
    if (zoneId) localSt orage. setItem(SITE.storageKey + ".miaZona", zoneI d);
    else localStorage.removeItem(SITE.storage Key + ".mia Zona") ;
  } catch (e) { /* storage non disponibile: la zona vale solo per la sessione */ }
}
/* Zona più vicina a una posiz ione GPS (dista nza approssimata su piano locale ). */
function nea rest Zone(lat, l ng) {
  const R = 6371, rad = Math.PI / 180, m = Math.cos(l at * ra d);
  let best = nul l, bd = Infinity;
  ZONES.forEach(z => {
    const dLat = (z.gps[0] - lat) * rad, dLng = (z.gps[1] - l ng) * rad;
    const d = R * Math.sqrt( dLat * dLat + ( dLng * m) * (dLng * m));
    if (d < bd) { bd = d; be st = z; }
  });
  return be st;
}

/* ---------------- Form recensione (scrittura e rettifica) ---------------- */
function criteriaFormHTML(cat, vals) {
  const v = va ls || {};
  const ra ngeFiel d = ( id, la bel, val ue) =>
    '<div class="fiel d" ><label for="' + id + '" >' + e sc(la bel) + '</la bel >' +
    '<div class="ra nge- wra p" ><input ty pe="ra nge" id="' + id + '" min=" 0" max =" 10" step=" 0.5" value="' + (val ue == nul l ? 5 : val ue) + '" a ria-describe dby="' + id + '-val" >' +
    '<spa n class="ra nge-val " id="' + id + '-val" a ria-live="polite" >' + fmt1( value == null ? 5 : val ue) + "</span></div ></ div >";
  let html = ra ngeFiel d(" rv-score", "Punteggio comple ssiv o del l'esperienza ( 0– 10) ", v.score );

    recensioni: vie Adm inR evie ws, espe rie nze: view Adm inEx periences, inv iti: vie wA dminI nvite s,
    utenti: vie wAdm inUse rs, e ntita: vie wA dminEntitie s, conte nuti: vie wAdm inConte nts,
    impostazioni: vie wAdm inSetting s, log: v iew AdminA udit, sicurezza: vie wAdm inSec urit y,
    ruoli: viewAdm inR ole s, demo: view AdminDemo
  };
  const fn = ma p[section] || viewA dminDa shboard;
  return fn();
}

function pa rseHa sh2() {
  const h = location.hash || "#/";
  let rest = h.charAt( 0) === "#" ? h.slice(1) : h;
  let frag = null;
  const hi = re st.index Of( "#");
  if (hi >= 0) { frag = re st.slice( hi + 1); rest = rest. slice( 0, hi) ; }
  const pa rts = re st.split(" ?");
  const seg s = part s[0]. spl it("/" ).filte r(Boolea n);
  const pa rams = {};
  if (pa rts[ 1]) pa rts[ 1].split("&" ).forEach(k v => {
    const p = kv.split( "=" );
    if (p[0]) params[ decode URIComponent( p[0])] = decode URICompone nt( p[1] || "");
  });
  return { seg s: segs, param s: pa rams, frag: frag } ;
}
